AGREEMENT BETWEEN
NORTHEAST ARIZONA TRAINING CENTER AND NA V AJO COUNTY
Shooting Range Berm Improvement Project
THIS AGREEMENT (“Agreement”) is entered into as of _______________, 2026 (“the 
Effective Date”) by and between NORTHEAST ARIZONA TRAINING CENTER, a non-profit  
corporation (“NATC”), acting by and through its Board of Directors, and NA V AJO COUNTY , a 
political subdivision of the State of Arizona (“County”), acting by and through its Board of  
Supervisors.
WHEREAS, NATC operates a firearms training range located at 1840 West Papermill  
Road, Taylor, Arizona, used for law enforcement training and certification; and
WHEREAS, the firearms range is utilized by various law enforcement agencies, including 
the Navajo County Sheriff’s Office, for training and certification purposes; and
WHEREAS, NATC has identified the need to raise and improve the existing shooting  
berm at the firearms range to meet applicable safety standards; and
WHEREAS, improvements to the berm will enhance safety for range users and for  
members of the public utilizing nearby recreational trails; and
WHEREAS, the NATC Governing Board has authorized funding in an amount not to  
exceed five thousand dollars ($5,000) to compensate the County for assistance with this project;  
and
WHEREAS, Navajo  County  Public  Works has  personnel  and  equipment  capable  of 
performing the necessary berm improvements; and
WHEREAS, County and NATC desire to cooperate in improving safety at the firearms  
training range.
NOW, THEREFORE, the parties hereby agree as follows:
I. PURPOSE
The purpose of this Agreement is to establish the responsibilities of the parties for the  
transport, placement, and grading of soil to raise the shooting berm at the NATC firearms  
range in order to meet applicable safety standards.
II. GENERAL AND MISCELLANEOUS PROVISIONS
a. Navajo County’s contributions are being made solely as an accommodation to  
NATC in recognition that the Project will benefit residents of Navajo County. The

<!-- page 378 -->
County will have no liability or responsibility to third parties in connection with  
the design or construction of the Project. NATC shall indemnify, defend, and hold 
harmless Navajo County, its officers, agents, and employees from and against any  
claims, damages, liabilities, or expenses arising out of or related to the condition of 
the NATC property where the work is performed, except to the extent caused by the 
negligence or willful misconduct of Navajo County.
b. NATC’s contributions are being made solely as an accommodation to the County  
in recognition that the Project will benefit residents of the Navajo County area as  
well as NATC students. NATC may provide construction inspections to ensure  
construction is completed in accordance with NATC’s requirements. 
c. NATC shall contribute an amount not to exceed five thousand dollars ($5,000), to  
compensate the County for services provided in connection with this project. NATC 
shall remit payment to Navajo County within thirty (30) days of receipt of an  
invoice from the County following completion of the work.
d. County shall contribute the remaining costs associated with the project, not to  
exceed five thousand dollars ($5,000), which will be funded through the County  
General  Fund  and  used  to  reimburse  Public  Works  for  labor  and  equipment 
expenses.
e. This Agreement is effective upon the date of the last signature below and shall  
remain operative until termination or expiration.
f. This Agreement is not intended to create any right, benefit, or trust, substantive or  
procedural,  enforceable  at  law  or  equity,  by  any  party  or  non-party  to  this 
Agreement against the County, its agencies, its officers, or any other person.
g. Termination  of  this  Agreement  will  not  affect  the  parties’  rights  and 
authorities necessary to perform the actions, functions, and/or services attendant  
to the execution, administration, and expiration or termination of this Agreement.  
Either party may terminate this Agreement with written notice to the other party.
h. None of the activities or information generated pursuant to this Agreement shall  
require either party to obligate or encumber funds or assets; although, either party  
may choose to do so of its own volition.
III. SCOPE OF WORK
Navajo County Public Works shall perform work associated with raising the shooting berm 
at the NATC firearms training range, which may include:
a. Transporting the soil from the Taylor Road Yard and/or utilizing soil currently  
located at the NATC site; and

<!-- page 379 -->
b. Placement and grading of soil to increase the height and stability of the existing  
berm; and
c. Operation of County heavy equipment necessary to complete the project; and
d. Labor  by  Navajo  County  Public  Works  personnel  required  to  complete  the 
project.
All work will occur at:
Northeast Arizona Training Center
1840 W. Papermill Road
Taylor, Arizona 85939
The work shall be coordinated with NATC representatives and scheduled at a mutually  
agreeable time.
IV . TERM
This Agreement shall remain in effect from the date of the signing of the Agreement until  
the completion of the project, unless otherwise amended, modified, or terminated by the  
parties in writing. This Agreement is also terminable in accordance with the provisions of  
A.R.S. §38-511, and that statute is hereby incorporated into the Agreement by reference.
V . ENTIRE AGREEMENT
This Agreement embodies the parties’ entire understanding with respect to its subject  
matter, superseding all prior understandings, whether oral or written, with respect to the  
same subject matter.
VI. AMENDMENTS
This Agreement may be amended (including any modification of the scope, funding, or  
responsibilities of the parties) only in a document that expressly refers to it and is executed 
and approved with the same formality by the parties. 
VII. SEVERABILITY
If any portion of this Agreement is found to be unenforceable by a court of competent  
jurisdiction, the remaining terms and provisions unaffected thereby shall remain in full  
force and effect.
VIII. ALTERNATIVE DISPUTE RESOLUTION
In the event of a disagreement between the parties regarding the interpretation of this  
Agreement or their respective obligations under this Agreement, the parties agree that they 
will first make efforts to resolve the matter through alternative dispute resolution prior to  
proceeding to litigation in court.

<!-- page 380 -->
IX. JURISDICTION AND VENUE
In the event of a dispute that the parties are unable to resolve through the use of an  
alternative dispute resolution process, it is agreed that the Courts of Navajo County,  
Arizona, shall have exclusive jurisdiction and be the appropriate venue for resolving the  
matter.
